text-batcave">[<a data-yga="{&quot;yInteractivityType&quot;:&quot;0&quot;,&quot;yLinkElement&quot;:&quot;context_link&quot;,&quot;yLinkText&quot;:&quot;FTNN新聞網&quot;,&quot;yModuleName&quot;:&quot;content-canvas&quot;}" data-ylk="slk:FTNN新聞網;elm:context_link;itc:0;sec:content-canvas;" href="https://tw.news.yahoo.com/tag/FTNN新聞網" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">FTNN新聞網</a>]記者何亞軒／綜合報導</p><p class="mb-module-gap break-words leading-[1.4] text-px20 lg:text-px18 lg:leading-[1.8] text-batcave">大和資本在日前出示報告，指出軸承大廠富世達（6805）將受惠於產品組合改善，生產效率提升，2026、2027每股盈餘（EPS）有望上調6%至7%，因此維持「買進」評等，目標價2315元；Factset同步調升，多位分析師上修富世達2026年EPS預估，中位數達到62.11元，目標價2080元。然而受到台股大盤影響，富世達今（9）日慘陷跌停板，終場收在1665元，大跌180元。</p><div class="bg-toast-background -mx-5 -mt-2 mb-3 pb-3 md:-mx-0 lg:mx-auto lg:w-[300px] lg:pb-0"><div class="text-px14 text-gandalf py-1 text-center leading-5">廣告</div><div class="mx-auto text-center min-w-[300px] min-h-[250px]" id="sda-inbody-7ec5e116-0401-3243-bc93-6416ffb41102-1-iframe"><div class="bg-dirty-seagull text-px12 text-dolphin mx-auto hidden items-center justify-center only:flex w-[300px] h-[250px]">廣告</div></div></div><figure class="mb-module-gap"><div class="mx-auto w-fit max-w-full"><img alt="今（9）日富世達慘陷跌停板，終場收在1665元，大跌180元。　（示意圖／pexels）" class="rounded-lg" fetchPriority="high" height="802" src="https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/27c05451ae7d9c9797b418fb1cafdb6810c5b693fdd49927461967aba221bd72/lightyear_networkapi/resizefill_w1200%3Bquality_80%3Bformat_webp/https%3A%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fzh-tw%2Fftnn_com_tw_939%2F156be3b0366fd1b4d797239312ee965f" width="1200"/><figcaption class="text-px14 text-dolphin mt-2 wrap-anywhere" style="max-width:1200px">今（9）日富世達慘陷跌停板，終場收在1665元，大跌180元。　（示意圖／pexels）</figcaption></div></figure><p class="mb-module-gap break-words leading-[1.4] text-px20 lg:text-px18 lg:leading-[1.8] text-batcave">根據財報資料顯示，富世達2025年第四季每股盈餘（EPS）為10.96元，年增82%、季增9%，雖略低於市場預期，但毛利率寫下29%，季增1.4個百分點，除了高於大和預估的27.6%，也高於市場共識的28.2%。</p><div class="recommendation-contents"><div class="bg-marshmallow mb-2 h-[30px] w-[80px] rounded-lg"></div><div class="no-scrollbar -mx-5 mb-5 flex overflow-hidden overflow-x-auto md:-mx-2 lg:mb-3.5"><div class="bg-marshmallow mr-2 ml-5 h-[94px] w-[calc(85%-28px)] flex-shrink-0 rounded-lg md:ml-2 md:w-[calc(85%-16px)] lg:w-[300px]"></div><div class="bg-marshmallow mx-2 h-[94px] w-[85%] flex-shrink-0 rounded-lg lg:w-[300px]"></div><div class="bg-marshmallow hidden h-[94px] w-[300px] flex-shrink-0 rounded-lg lg:block"></div></div></div><p class="mb-module-gap break-words leading-[1.4] text-px20 lg:text-px18 lg:leading-[1.8] text-batcave">大和指出，富世達法說會呈現出正面訊息，對富世達2026年的營收與毛利率持樂觀看法，預期伺服器營收年成長至少達1倍，維持富世達買進評等，目標價2315元。</p><p class="mb-module-gap break-words leading-[1.4] text-px20 lg:text-px18 lg:leading-[1.8] text-batcave">除此之外，Factse也出示最新報告，共10位分析師對富世達2026年EPS進行預估，其中位數由59.26元上修至62.11元，最高估值66.31元，最低估值41.9元，預估目標價為2080元。</p><p class="mb-module-gap break-words leading-[1.4] text-px20 lg:text-px18 lg:leading-[1.8] text-batcave">不過儘管外資對於富世達後市看好，今日富世達受到美股影響，終場仍鎖死跌停1665元，大跌180元，成交量達逾1200張。</p><p class="mb-module-gap break-words leading-[1.4] text-px20 lg:text-px18 lg:leading-[1.8] text-batcave"><strong>◎《FTNN新聞網》提醒您：本資料僅供參考，投資人應獨立判斷，審慎評估並自負投資風險。</strong></p><p class="read-more-vendor mb-module-gap break-words leading-[1.4] text-px20 lg:text-px18 lg:leading-[1.8] text-batcave"><span>更多FTNN新聞網報導</span><br/><a wrap-anywhere">黃仁勳回歸輝達執行長後，該公司單日大漲31.48%，台股今（24）天也受到激勵，猛漲3310.46點，收在40122.35點。然而，周二輝達盤前下跌超過5%，部分AI企業也出現下滑的趨勢。台指期夜盤稍早雖延續漲勢，一度上漲576點，報40900點，隨後卻一路震盪走低，一度暴跌988點，報39336點，失守4萬點大關，截至晚間8點左右，下跌624點，暫報39700點。</p><div 2018也回來了！高盛警告：美股暴漲只是熊市反彈</a></h3><p class="text-px16 text-dolphin my-1 line-clamp-2 wrap-anywhere">黃仁勳回任輝達執行長帶動美股強彈、那指100漲約7%，但高盛指這波更像空頭回補的熊市反彈。 高盛稱信用市場落後股市，投資級公司債利差僅擴大約13個基點，2年期通膨交換利率降至2.09%。 花旗拿2026年對照2018年，稱景氣循環股、科技股與醫療保健轉弱，市場壓力恐持續擴散。</p><div class="text-px12 text-dolphin mt-1 font-normal flex"><span class="line-clamp-1">鉅亨網 ・ 4 小時前</span> ・ <span class="text-px12 text-dolphin ference-stream\":{\"mode\":\"stream-twhk-article-super-app-ios-d\",\"placement\":\"taboola-stream-articled\",\"region\":\"index\",\"targetType\":\"mix\"},\"stream-recommended-articles\":{\"mode\":\"stream-twhk-article-super-app-ios-c\",\"placement\":\"taboola-stream-articlec\",\"region\":\"index\",\"targetType\":\"mix\"}},\"appNames\":[\"super\",\"superhk\",\"ytwstock\",\"twnews\",\"hknews\"],\"desktop\":{\"pencil-read-more\":{\"mode\":\"pencil-twhk-article-revamp-a-v2\",\"placement\":\"taboola-pencil-articlea-v2\",\"region\":\"index\",\"targetType\":\"mix\"},\"stream-preference-stream\":{\"mode\":\"stream-twhk-article-revamp-b\",\"placement\":\"taboola-stream-articleb\",\"region\":\"index\",\"targetType\":\"mix\"},\"stream-preference-stream-large\":{\"mode\":\"stream-twhk-article-revamp-e\",\"placement\":\"taboola-stream-articlee\",\"region\":\"index\",\"targetType\":\"mix\"},\"stream-recommended-articles\":{\"mode\":\"stream-twhk-article-revamp-a\",\"placement\":\"taboola-stream-articlea\",\"region\":\"index\",\"targetType\":\"mix\"}},\"smartphone\":{\"pencil-read-more\":{\"mode\":\"pencil-twhk-article-revamp-b\",\"placement\":\"taboola-pencil-articleb\",\"region\":\"index\",\"targetType\":\"mix\"},\"stream-preference-stream\":{\"mode\":\"stream-twhk-article-revamp-d\",\"placement\":\"taboola-stream-articled\",\"region\":\"index\",\"targetType\":\"mix\"},\"stream-recommended-articles\":{\"mode\":\"stream-twhk-article-revamp-c\",\"placement\":\"taboola-stream-articlec\",\"region\":\"index\",\"targetType\":\"mix\"}}}},\"pageName\":\"articlePage\",\"pageState\":{\"article\":{\"data\":{\"bodyBlocks\":[{\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":\"left\",\"characterCount\":19},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"1\",\"parentNodeId\":null,\"subtype\":null,\"text\":null,\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"uuid\":null,\"content\":[{\"attrs\":{},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"2\",\"parentNodeId\":\"1\",\"subtype\":null,\"text\":\"[\",\"type\":\"text\",\"uuid\":null},{\"attrs\":{\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://tw.news.yahoo.com/tag/FTNN新聞網\",\"target\":\"_blank\",\"rel\":\"\",\"class\":\"link\",\"isValidAffiliate\":null,\"isDoNotAffiliateLink\":false,\"dataOriginalLink\":null,\"data-autolinker-wiki-id\":null,\"dataYlk\":{\"slk\":\"FTNN新聞網\",\"elm\":\"context_link\",\"itc\":\"0\",\"sec\":\"content-canvas\"}}}]},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"3\",\"parentNodeId\":\"1\",\"subtype\":null,\"text\":\"FTNN新聞網\",\"type\":\"text\",\"uuid\":null},{\"attrs\":{},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"4\",\"parentNodeId\":\"1\",\"subtype\":null,\"text\":\"]記者何亞軒／綜合報導\",\"type\":\"text\",\"uuid\":null}]},{\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":\"left\",\"characterCount\":189},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"5\",\"parentNodeId\":null,\"subtype\":null,\"text\":null,\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"uuid\":null,\"content\":[{\"attrs\":{},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"6\",\"parentNodeId\":\"5\",\"subtype\":null,\"text\":\"大和資本在日前出示報告，指出軸承大廠富世達（6805）將受惠於產品組合改善，生產效率提升，2026、2027每股盈餘（EPS）有望上調6%至7%，因此維持「買進」評等，目標價2315元；Factset同步調升，多位分析師上修富世達2026年EPS預估，中位數達到62.11元，目標價2080元。然而受到台股大盤影響，富世達今（9）日慘陷跌停板，終場收在1665元，大跌180元。\",\"type\":\"text\",\"uuid\":null}]},{\"attrs\":\"$undefined\",\"hydratedAsset\":\"$undefined\",\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"7\",\"parentNodeId\":null,\"subtype\":null,\"text\":null,\"type\":\"providerImage\",\"uuid\":\"0b19eadf-0551-3e83-8f4e-bfb4af85d850\",\"transformed\":{\"alt\":\"今（9）日富世達慘陷跌停板，終場收在1665元，大跌180元。　（示意圖／pexels）\",\"caption\":\"今（9）日富世達慘陷跌停板，終場收在1665元，大跌180元。　（示意圖／pexels）\",\"credit\":\"\",\"href\":\"\",\"size\":{\"original\":{\"height\":668,\"url\":\"https://media.zenfs.com/zh-tw/ftnn_com_tw_939/156be3b0366fd1b4d797239312ee965f\",\"width\":1000},\"resized\":{\"height\":802,\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/27c05451ae7d9c9797b418fb1cafdb6810c5b693fdd49927461967aba221bd72/lightyear_networkapi/resizefill_w1200%3Bquality_80%3Bformat_webp/https%3A%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fzh-tw%2Fftnn_com_tw_939%2F156be3b0366fd1b4d797239312ee965f\",\"width\":1200}}}},{\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":\"left\",\"characterCount\":106},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"8\",\"parentNodeId\":null,\"subtype\":null,\"text\":null,\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"uuid\":null,\"content\":[{\"attrs\":{},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"9\",\"parentNodeId\":\"8\",\"subtype\":null,\"text\":\"根據財報資料顯示，富世達2025年第四季每股盈餘（EPS）為10.96元，年增82%、季增9%，雖略低於市場預期，但毛利率寫下29%，季增1.4個百分點，除了高於大和預估的27.6%，也高於市場共識的28.2%。\",\"type\":\"text\",\"uuid\":null}]},{\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":\"left\",\"characterCount\":76},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"10\",\"parentNodeId\":null,\"subtype\":null,\"text\":null,\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"uuid\":null,\"content\":[{\"attrs\":{},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"11\",\"parentNodeId\":\"10\",\"subtype\":null,\"text\":\"大和指出，富世達法說會呈現出正面訊息，對富世達2026年的營收與毛利率持樂觀看法，預期伺服器營收年成長至少達1倍，維持富世達買進評等，目標價2315元。\",\"type\":\"text\",\"uuid\":null}]},{\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":\"left\",\"characterCount\":97},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"12\",\"parentNodeId\":null,\"subtype\":null,\"text\":null,\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"uuid\":null,\"content\":[{\"attrs\":{},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"13\",\"parentNodeId\":\"12\",\"subtype\":null,\"text\":\"除此之外，Factse也出示最新報告，共10位分析師對富世達2026年EPS進行預估，其中位數由59.26元上修至62.11元，最高估值66.31元，最低估值41.9元，預估目標價為2080元。\",\"type\":\"text\",\"uuid\":null}]},{\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":\"left\",\"characterCount\":59},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"14\",\"parentNodeId\":null,\"subtype\":null,\"text\":null,\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"uuid\":null,\"content\":[{\"attrs\":{},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"15\",\"parentNodeId\":\"14\",\"subtype\":null,\"text\":\"不過儘管外資對於富世達後市看好，今日富世達受到美股影響，終場仍鎖死跌停1665元，大跌180元，成交量達逾1200張。\",\"type\":\"text\",\"uuid\":null}]},{\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":\"left\",\"characterCount\":43},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"16\",\"parentNodeId\":null,\"subtype\":null,\"text\":null,\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"uuid\":null,\"content\":[{\"attrs\":{\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"bold\"}]},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"17\",\"parentNodeId\":\"16\",\"subtype\":null,\"text\":\"◎《FTNN新聞網》提醒您：本資料僅供參考，投資人應獨立判斷，審慎評估並自負投資風險。\",\"type\":\"text\",\"uuid\":null}]},{\"attrs\":{\"textAlign\":\"left\",\"class\":\"read-more-vendor\",\"characterCount\":131},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"18\",\"parentNodeId\":null,\"subtype\":null,\"text\":null,\"type\":\"paragraph\",\"uuid\":null,\"content\":[{\"attrs\":{},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"19\",\"parentNodeId\":\"18\",\"subtype\":null,\"text\":\"更多FTNN新聞網報導\",\"type\":\"text\",\"uuid\":null},{\"attrs\":{},\"hydratedAsset\":null,\"id\":null,\"items\":null,\"nodeId\":\"20\",\"parentNodeId\":\"18\",\"subtype\":null,\"text\":null,\"type\":\"hardBreak\",\"uuid\":null},{\"attrs\":{\"marks\":[{\"type\":\"link\",\"attrs\":{\"href\":\"https://www.ftnn.com.tw/news/528547?utm_source=yahoo\u0026utm_medium=referral\u0026utm_campaign=rss\u0026utm_content=528547\",\"target\":\"_blank\",\"rel\":\"noopener noreferrer 8B%B3%E5%9B%9E%E6%AD%B8%E8%82%A1%E6%B0%91%E5%96%8A-%E5%87%8D%E8%92%9C-%E6%B2%88%E6%98%A5%E8%8F%AF%E8%AA%8D-%E8%B5%B7%E8%B2%AA%E5%BF%B5-%E5%B8%B3%E9%9D%A2%E8%90%BD%E5%B7%AE%E8%BF%91%E5%85%AB%E4%BD%8D%E6%95%B8-071504225.html\"},\"canvass\":{\"count\":38},\"categoryLabel\":\"$undefined\",\"clickThroughUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.stock.yahoo.com/news/%E9%BB%83%E4%BB%81%E5%8B%B3%E5%9B%9E%E6%AD%B8%E8%82%A1%E6%B0%91%E5%96%8A-%E5%87%8D%E8%92%9C-%E6%B2%88%E6%98%A5%E8%8F%AF%E8%AA%8D-%E8%B5%B7%E8%B2%AA%E5%BF%B5-%E5%B8%B3%E9%9D%A2%E8%90%BD%E5%B7%AE%E8%BF%91%E5%85%AB%E4%BD%8D%E6%95%B8-071504225.html\"},\"color\":\"$undefined\",\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"duration\":\"$undefined\",\"id\":\"48c4018b-43be-3cc0-afaa-263333355889\",\"isHosted\":true,\"label\":\"$undefined\",\"liveVideo\":\"$undefined\",\"mabLogString\":\"$undefined\",\"originalTitle\":\"黃仁勳回歸股民喊「凍蒜」！沈春華認「起貪念」帳面落差近八位數\",\"photoVariants\":\"$undefined\",\"provider\":{\"displayName\":\"民視財經網\",\"id\":\"$undefined\",\"logo\":{\"resolutions\":[]},\"sourceId\":\"$undefined\",\"url\":\"$undefined\"},\"providerContentUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"pubDate\":\"2026-11-24T15:36:36Z\",\"summary\":\"財經中心／高淑婷報導輝達（NVIDIA）昨（23）日宣布，董事會一致同意讓黃仁勳回任執行長，美國科技股應聲走強，輝達股價收盤大漲逾3成。台股今（24）日也跟著反彈，終場大漲逾3300點，收復40000點大關。資深媒體人沈春華也在臉書直呼「選舉倒數四天，讓股民大喊『凍蒜』的，竟然是黃仁勳」。她也坦言，過去一年眼看台股一路上漲，自己也動了貪念加碼股市，這波震盪讓她從帳面獲利轉為虧損，前後相差近八位數。\",\"tags\":[],\"thumbnail\":{\"crop\":\"$undefined\",\"originalHeight\":\"$undefined\",\"originalUrl\":\"https://media.zenfs.com/ko/ftvn_finance_535/93e30371ff25faa9a2af76a954fab37c.jpg\",\"originalWidth\":\"$undefined\",\"resolutions\":[{\"height\":280,\"tag\":\"498x280\",\"transformLabel\":\"498x280\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/66CA83C36D9AA9030F9622CAB7E37D1DF79158203AAFBF3AAE617BC5A3CA5328/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w498_h280_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fftvn_finance_535%2F93e30371ff25faa9a2af76a954fab37c.jpg\",\"width\":498},{\"height\":184,\"tag\":\"184x184\",\"transformLabel\":\"184x184\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/772DE513371BB0B26C5EF57098C8D6A0E53A3A7D02AAA7A235709952FAD0B4F4/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w184_h184_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fftvn_finance_535%2F93e30371ff25faa9a2af76a954fab37c.jpg\",\"width\":184}]},\"title\":\"黃仁勳回歸股民喊「凍蒜」！沈春華認「起貪念」帳面落差近八位數\"},\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"id\":\"48c4018b-43be-3cc0-afaa-263333355889\"},{\"ad\":true},{\"content\":{\"author\":\"$undefined\",\"canonicalUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.news.yahoo.com/%E7%AB%B9%E5%8C%97%E5%90%8A%E8%BB%8A%E5%A4%A7%E7%8E%8B%E8%B5%B4%E4%B8%AD%E5%9C%8B-%E5%8D%83%E4%BA%BA%E6%AD%A1%E5%91%BC-%E7%95%AB%E9%9D%A2%E6%9B%9D%E5%85%89-4%E5%8F%B0%E5%B7%A8%E5%9E%8B%E5%90%8A%E8%BB%8A%E5%B1%95%E5%BE%8C%E5%85%A8%E9%81%8B%E5%9B%9E%E5%8F%B0-132700195.html\"},\"canvass\":{\"count\":84},\"categoryLabel\":\"$undefined\",\"clickThroughUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.news.yahoo.com/%E7%AB%B9%E5%8C%97%E5%90%8A%E8%BB%8A%E5%A4%A7%E7%8E%8B%E8%B5%B4%E4%B8%AD%E5%9C%8B-%E5%8D%83%E4%BA%BA%E6%AD%A1%E5%91%BC-%E7%95%AB%E9%9D%A2%E6%9B%9D%E5%85%89-4%E5%8F%B0%E5%B7%A8%E5%9E%8B%E5%90%8A%E8%BB%8A%E5%B1%95%E5%BE%8C%E5%85%A8%E9%81%8B%E5%9B%9E%E5%8F%B0-132700195.html\"},\"color\":\"$undefined\",\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"duration\":\"$undefined\",\"id\":\"a3d6e0d3-ba5f-347f-bf00-46e89331aba3\",\"isHosted\":true,\"label\":\"$undefined\",\"liveVideo\":\"$undefined\",\"mabLogString\":\"$undefined\",\"originalTitle\":\"竹北吊車大王赴中國！「千人歡呼」畫面曝光　4台巨型吊車展後全運回台\",\"photoVariants\":\"$undefined\",\"provider\":{\"displayName\":\"三立新聞網 setn.com\",\"id\":\"$undefined\",\"logo\":{\"resolutions\":[]},\"sourceId\":\"$undefined\",\"url\":\"$undefined\"},\"providerContentUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"pubDate\":\"2026-11-23T13:27:00Z\",\"summary\":\"竹北吊車大王胡漢龑日前親赴中國湖州參加三一重工大客戶峰會，穿招牌東北大花套裝亮相引發關注。他現場見證無人自駕與雙機全遙控吊起250噸重物等震撼科技，並宣布啟德新購的1200噸、800噸等4台巨型吊車全數參展，展後將運回台灣添生力軍，展現強大重機實力。\",\"tags\":[],\"thumbnail\":{\"crop\":\"$undefined\",\"originalHeight\":\"$undefined\",\"originalUrl\":\"https://media.zenfs.com/ko/setn.com.tw/833c5c4a727d0757567066e1900aa8a5.jpg\",\"originalWidth\":\"$undefined\",\"resolutions\":[{\"height\":280,\"tag\":\"498x280\",\"transformLabel\":\"498x280\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/20E6026AAAC3EFB80131718C920A2393F8269C7814D0920F69329E038B94D192/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w498_h280_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fsetn.com.tw%2F833c5c4a727d0757567066e1900aa8a5.jpg\",\"width\":498},{\"height\":184,\"tag\":\"184x184\",\"transformLabel\":\"184x184\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/1EF88668B1AF8F4CCBA220B09A694AC7C4B403F2EADA133299697A5158CD5CE8/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w184_h184_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fsetn.com.tw%2F833c5c4a727d0757567066e1900aa8a5.jpg\",\"width\":184}]},\"title\":\"竹北吊車大王赴中國！「千人歡呼」畫面曝光　4台巨型吊車展後全運回台\"},\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"id\":\"a3d6e0d3-ba5f-347f-bf00-46e89331aba3\"},{\"content\":{\"author\":\"$undefined\",\"canonicalUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.stock.yahoo.com/news/%E5%8F%B0%E9%81%94%E9%9B%BB%E8%91%A3%E5%BA%A7%E7%94%B3%E8%AE%93%E8%BF%91%E5%8D%8A%E6%95%B8%E6%8C%81%E8%82%A1-%E5%B8%82%E5%80%BC%E9%80%BE300%E5%84%84%E5%85%83-%E5%85%AC%E5%8F%B8%E6%8F%AD%E5%8E%9F%E5%9B%A0-141800979.html\"},\"canvass\":{\"count\":9},\"categoryLabel\":\"$undefined\",\"clickThroughUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.stock.yahoo.com/news/%E5%8F%B0%E9%81%94%E9%9B%BB%E8%91%A3%E5%BA%A7%E7%94%B3%E8%AE%93%E8%BF%91%E5%8D%8A%E6%95%B8%E6%8C%81%E8%82%A1-%E5%B8%82%E5%80%BC%E9%80%BE300%E5%84%84%E5%85%83-%E5%85%AC%E5%8F%B 但一切都變了\",\"photoVariants\":\"$undefined\",\"provider\":{\"displayName\":\"中時新聞網\",\"id\":\"$undefined\",\"logo\":{\"resolutions\":[]},\"sourceId\":\"$undefined\",\"url\":\"$undefined\"},\"providerContentUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"pubDate\":\"2026-11-24T20:10:00Z\",\"summary\":\"輝達執行長黃仁勳的去職，和國安有關，然而他也是因國安而回歸。關鍵人物是美國總統川普，他認為美中競爭關鍵在AI（川普稱SI），而黃仁勳是不可或缺的重要人物。誠如特斯拉執行長馬斯克批評輝達，讓黃仁勳離開如同公牛隊連勝時踢出喬丹。\",\"tags\":[],\"thumbnail\":{\"crop\":\"$undefined\",\"originalHeight\":\"$undefined\",\"originalUrl\":\"https://media.zenfs.com/zh-tw/chinatimes.com.tw/2725c0927928c8131a7bb4eb288462e5.jpg_up.jpg\",\"originalWidth\":\"$undefined\",\"resolutions\":[{\"height\":280,\"tag\":\"498x280\",\"transformLabel\":\"498x280\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/728BFBF24A5CF7AEAE7D39D2FA30E46703C9C9B27411DC3AF45D9FA995A91D19/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w498_h280_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fzh-tw%2Fchinatimes.com.tw%2F2725c0927928c8131a7bb4eb288462e5.jpg_up.jpg\",\"width\":498},{\"height\":184,\"tag\":\"184x184\",\"transformLabel\":\"184x184\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/F6C464E5CB9F87E05AAD9CB09BB269143C69B73CBBE1F9B1C9155B426D2EC5E2/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w184_h184_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fzh-tw%2Fchinatimes.com.tw%2F2725c0927928c8131a7bb4eb288462e5.jpg_up.jpg\",\"width\":184}]},\"title\":\"黃仁勳回歸 但一切都變了\"},\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"id\":\"b82bb40e-14f2-3f27-b9d0-74a05b112da5\"},{\"ad\":true},{\"content\":{\"author\":\"$undefined\",\"canonicalUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.news.yahoo.com/%E9%BB%83%E4%BB%81%E5%8B%B3%E5%90%91%E8%BC%9D%E9%81%94%E5%93%A1%E5%B7%A5%E8%87%B4%E6%AD%89-%E5%9D%A6%E6%89%BF%E9%81%95%E5%8F%8D%E8%A1%8C%E7%82%BA%E6%BA%96%E5%89%87-%E6%8E%A5%E5%8F%97%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E6%B2%BB%E7%99%82-222220257.html\"},\"canvass\":{\"count\":63},\"categoryLabel\":\"$undefined\",\"clickThroughUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.news.yahoo.com/%E9%BB%83%E4%BB%81%E5%8B%B3%E5%90%91%E8%BC%9D%E9%81%94%E5%93%A1%E5%B7%A5%E8%87%B4%E6%AD%89-%E5%9D%A6%E6%89%BF%E9%81%95%E5%8F%8D%E8%A1%8C%E7%82%BA%E6%BA%96%E5%89%87-%E6%8E%A5%E5%8F%97%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E6%B2%BB%E7%99%82-222220257.html\"},\"color\":\"$undefined\",\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"duration\":\"$undefined\",\"id\":\"28123b61-abc5-33eb-8fae-f3f11cd8fcd9\",\"isHosted\":true,\"label\":\"$undefined\",\"liveVideo\":\"$undefined\",\"mabLogString\":\"$undefined\",\"originalTitle\":\"黃仁勳向輝達員工致歉  坦承違反行為準則、接受心理治療\",\"photoVariants\":\"$undefined\",\"provider\":{\"displayName\":\"中央社\",\"id\":\"$undefined\",\"logo\":{\"resolutions\":[]},\"sourceId\":\"$undefined\",\"url\":\"$undefined\"},\"providerContentUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"pubDate\":\"2026-11-24T22:22:20Z\",\"summary\":\"（中央社記者張欣瑜舊金山24日專電）黃仁勳今天在輝達全體員工大會為自己違反公司行為準則向員工道歉，透露目前每天接受心理治療，同時表示自己並非是「科技圈大佬」能夠凌駕法律之上，也否認自己的復職是川普向輝達董事會施壓的結果。\",\"tags\":[],\"thumbnail\":{\"crop\":\"$undefined\",\"originalHeight\":\"$undefined\",\"originalUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"originalWidth\":\"$undefined\",\"resolutions\":[]},\"title\":\"黃仁勳向輝達員工致歉  坦承違反行為準則、接受心理治療\"},\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"id\":\"28123b61-abc5-33eb-8fae-f3f11cd8fcd9\"},{\"content\":{\"author\":\"$undefined\",\"canonicalUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.news.yahoo.com/%E5%BF%AB%E8%A8%8A-%E5%B7%9D%E6%99%AE%E9%AC%86%E5%8F%A3-%E5%BF%85%E8%A6%81%E6%99%82%E9%A1%98%E6%84%8F-%E8%B5%A6%E5%85%8D%E9%BB%83%E4%BB%81%E5%8B%B3-%E8%80%83%E6%85%AE%E5%85%A5%E8%82%A1%E8%BC%9D%E9%81%94-000700375.html\"},\"canvass\":{\"count\":216},\"categoryLabel\":\"$undefined\",\"clickThroughUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.news.yahoo.com/%E5%BF%AB%E8%A8%8A-%E5%B7%9D%E6%99%AE%E9%AC%86%E5%8F%A3-%E5%BF%85%E8%A6%81%E6%99%82%E9%A1%98%E6%84%8F-%E8%B5%A6%E5%85%8D%E9%BB%83%E4%BB%81%E5%8B%B3-%E8%80%83%E6%85%AE%E5%85%A5%E8%82%A1%E8%BC%9D%E9%81%94-000700375.html\"},\"color\":\"$undefined\",\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"duration\":\"$undefined\",\"id\":\"98d11776-4456-34f5-bde7-10e794289c6f\",\"isHosted\":true,\"label\":\"$undefined\",\"liveVideo\":\"$undefined\",\"mabLogString\":\"$undefined\",\"originalTitle\":\"川普鬆口！必要時願意「赦免黃仁勳」、考慮入股輝達\",\"photoVariants\":\"$undefined\",\"provider\":{\"displayName\":\"三立新聞網 setn.com\",\"id\":\"$undefined\",\"logo\":{\"resolutions\":[]},\"sourceId\":\"$undefined\",\"url\":\"$undefined\"},\"providerContentUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"pubDate\":\"2026-11-25T00:07:00Z\",\"summary\":\"輝達執行長黃仁勳重返職位，國安爭議持續延燒。美國總統川普表示，若有必要不排除動用總統赦免權，並呼籲黃仁勳下週赴國會作證時說實話。此外，川普也對美國政府入股輝達持開放態度，引發各界高度關注。儘管川普公開力挺，國會兩黨議員仍要求輝達針對晶片出口與國安問題說明，黃仁勳的國會聽證成為關鍵焦點。\",\"tags\":[],\"thumbnail\":{\"crop\":\"$undefined\",\"originalHeight\":\"$undefined\",\"originalUrl\":\"https://media.zenfs.com/ko/setn.com.tw/7953e93a5eaa5cafe537276d3e003d1b.jpg\",\"originalWidth\":\"$undefined\",\"resolutions\":[{\"height\":280,\"tag\":\"498x280\",\"transformLabel\":\"498x280\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/08369C9CE65D688A5DB6C88CD2D242C2B55F48F8F713AB086770134EA49A59BB/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w498_h280_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fsetn.com.tw%2F7953e93a5eaa5cafe537276d3e003d1b.jpg\",\"width\":498},{\"height\":184,\"tag\":\"184x184\",\"transformLabel\":\"184x184\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/D09CCF586727E62CD7B136579B77D623B8219941DCF5F9C9CE4A5332FA3734E6/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w184_h184_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fsetn.com.tw%2F7953e93a5eaa5cafe537276d3e003d1b.jpg\",\"width\":184}]},\"title\":\"川普鬆口！必要時願意「赦免黃仁勳」、考慮入股輝達\"},\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"id\":\"98d11776-4456-34f5-bde7-10e794289c6f\"},{\"content\":{\"author\":\"$undefined\",\"canonicalUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.news.yahoo.com/%E6%9C%80%E4%BD%8E%E5%B7%A5%E8%B3%87%E6%98%8E%E5%B9%B4%E8%AA%BF%E6%BC%B2%E6%95%88%E6%87%89-%E5%8B%9E%E9%80%80%E7%B4%9A%E8%B7%9D%E8%AA%BF%E6%95%B4-135%E8%90%AC%E5%90%8D%E5%8B%9E%E5%B7%A5%E9%80%80%E4%BC%91%E9%87%91%E8%AE%8A%E5%A4%9A-223234566.html\"},\"canvass\":{\"count\":21},\"categoryLabel\":\"$undefined\",\"clickThroughUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.news.yahoo.com/%E6%9C%80%E4%BD%8E%E5%B7%A5%E8%B3%87%E6%98%8E%E5%B9%B4%E8%AA%BF%E6%BC%B2%E6%95%88%E6%87%89-%E5%8B%9E%E9%80%80%E7%B4%9A%E8%B7%9D%E8%AA%BF%E6%95%B4-135% 135萬名勞工退休金變多\",\"photoVariants\":\"$undefined\",\"provider\":{\"displayName\":\"聯合新聞網\",\"id\":\"$undefined\",\"logo\":{\"resolutions\":[]},\"sourceId\":\"$undefined\",\"url\":\"$undefined\"},\"providerContentUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"pubDate\":\"2026-11-24T22:32:34Z\",\"summary\":\"2027年元旦起，最低工資月薪將調漲至30,900元。配合最低工資調整，勞動部公告「勞工退休金月提繳分級表」，現行第26級月提繳工資，將由30,300元，修正為30,900元，同樣自2027年1月1日起生效。另配合修正現行第27級實際工資級距下限，其他級距維持不變。\",\"tags\":[],\"thumbnail\":{\"crop\":\"$undefined\",\"originalHeight\":\"$undefined\",\"originalUrl\":\"https://media.zenfs.com/ko/udn.com/497bfaac7beb7505e8849954968ef33b.jpg\",\"originalWidth\":\"$undefined\",\"resolutions\":[{\"height\":280,\"tag\":\"498x280\",\"transformLabel\":\"498x280\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/10DE2A159DE5F4C44D833E112DF1667C576E0CED60CE28F33D266527B7AAB990/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w498_h280_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fudn.com%2F497bfaac7beb7505e8849954968ef33b.jpg\",\"width\":498},{\"height\":184,\"tag\":\"184x184\",\"transformLabel\":\"184x184\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/32E324DB1A2D1B9B8D65945F1DEF97BB7ABD1FC54B5B3CD6D10BF96E68F82793/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w184_h184_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fudn.com%2F497bfaac7beb7505e8849954968ef33b.jpg\",\"width\":184}]},\"title\":\"最低工資明年調漲效應…勞退級距調整 135萬名勞工退休金變多\"},\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"id\":\"2e53bcd4-1f04-3016-88f9-0cda085721ae\"},{\"ad\":true},{\"content\":{\"author\":\"$undefined\",\"canonicalUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.news.yahoo.com/%E5%8F%B0%E8%82%A1%E6%97%A9%E7%9B%A4%E9%87%8D%E6%8C%AB%E9%80%BE3100%E9%BB%9E%E8%B7%8C%E7%A0%B4%E5%B9%B4%E7%B7%9A-%E5%8F%B0%E7%A9%8D%E9%9B%BB%E8%B7%8C190%E5%85%83-021721364.html\"},\"canvass\":{\"count\":146},\"categoryLabel\":\"$undefined\",\"clickThroughUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.news.yahoo.com/%E5%8F%B0%E8%82%A1%E6%97%A9%E7%9B%A4%E9%87%8D%E6%8C%AB%E9%80%BE3100%E9%BB%9E%E8%B7%8C%E7%A0%B4%E5%B9%B4%E7%B7%9A-%E5%8F%B0%E7%A9%8D%E9%9B%BB%E8%B7%8C190%E5%85%83-021721364.html\"},\"color\":\"$undefined\",\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"duration\":\"$undefined\",\"id\":\"e8a92207-4484-4ac0-b225-89c83c41f60d\",\"isHosted\":true,\"label\":\"$undefined\",\"liveVideo\":\"$undefined\",\"mabLogString\":\"$undefined\",\"originalTitle\":\"台股早盤重挫逾3100點跌破年線 台積電跌190元\",\"photoVariants\":\"$undefined\",\"provider\":{\"displayName\":\"Yahoo即時新聞\",\"id\":\"$undefined\",\"logo\":{\"resolutions\":[]},\"sourceId\":\"$undefined\",\"url\":\"$undefined\"},\"providerContentUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"pubDate\":\"2026-11-25T01:06:50Z\",\"summary\":\"由於人工智慧（AI）熱潮前景不明，科技類股再次遭投資人拋售，美國股市四大指數多收低。台北股市今天（25日）早盤重挫逾3100點，加權指數最低為37010.49點，4萬點大關失守，跌破年線約39554點。台積電最多跌190元至2040元。\",\"tags\":[],\"thumbnail\":{\"crop\":\"$undefined\",\"originalHeight\":\"$undefined\",\"originalUrl\":\"https://media.zenfs.com/zh-tw/cna.com.tw/6d4b91972ecd348193a27fe4c10a482d.jpg\",\"originalWidth\":\"$undefined\",\"resolutions\":[{\"height\":280,\"tag\":\"498x280\",\"transformLabel\":\"498x280\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/893F12B05FD681425BD38B9ED47017EA4B4F94242DB147868BA758CF037F8B12/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w498_h280_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fzh-tw%2Fcna.com.tw%2F6d4b91972ecd348193a27fe4c10a482d.jpg\",\"width\":498},{\"height\":184,\"tag\":\"184x184\",\"transformLabel\":\"184x184\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/8783C6C44DDAAEEF3C4991E4619CE7F236745B275A2517D0A49A17AF71A426B4/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w184_h184_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fzh-tw%2Fcna.com.tw%2F6d4b91972ecd348193a27fe4c10a482d.jpg\",\"width\":184}]},\"title\":\"台股早盤重挫逾3100點跌破年線 setn.com\",\"id\":\"$undefined\",\"logo\":{\"resolutions\":[]},\"sourceId\":\"$undefined\",\"url\":\"$undefined\"},\"providerContentUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"pubDate\":\"2026-11-24T22:28:00Z\",\"summary\":\"美股週二（24日）主要指數漲跌互見，前一交易日由黃仁勳回任輝達執行長帶動的AI股反彈行情未能延續，加上美國總統川普新一輪關稅計畫及聯準會（Fed）升息疑慮，科技股再度遭遇沉重賣壓，半導體及記憶體族群成為重災區。費半重挫7.88%，台指期夜盤大跌1842點，台股今（25）日開盤恐面臨4萬點保衛戰。\",\"tags\":[],\"thumbnail\":{\"crop\":\"$undefined\",\"originalHeight\":\"$undefined\",\"originalUrl\":\"https://media.zenfs.com/ko/setn.com.tw/934d24768f898d5ad511b0235c610fc4.jpg\",\"originalWidth\":\"$undefined\",\"resolutions\":[{\"height\":280,\"tag\":\"498x280\",\"transformLabel\":\"498x280\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/35239DB0177E3DF4FF7C964A6ED8DC34EF12414BB4D331E9EBE04760EBF5A740/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w498_h280_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fsetn.com.tw%2F934d24768f898d5ad511b0235c610fc4.jpg\",\"width\":498},{\"height\":184,\"tag\":\"184x184\",\"transformLabel\":\"184x184\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/D0DF6000454A4570C2DD454947184AC3CBE716DFB89EA9C07539E752AF141DDF/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w184_h184_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fsetn.com.tw%2F934d24768f898d5ad511b0235c610fc4.jpg\",\"width\":184}]},\"title\":\"【盤前】川普關稅再起費半崩近8%！台指夜盤殺1842點　台股4萬點告急\"},\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"id\":\"f1928ffd-e8ca-30a0-8cbe-b4d9ac5586aa\"},{\"content\":{\"author\":\"$undefined\",\"canonicalUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.stock.yahoo.com/news/%E5%B0%8F%E4%BA%94%E6%9C%AA%E8%AD%89%E5%AF%A6%E5%8F%88%E5%82%B3%E6%9C%89%E5%B0%8F%E5%85%AD%E5%B0%8F%E4%B8%83-wsj-%E9%BB%83%E4%BB%81%E5%8B%B3%E5%BE%A9%E8%81%B7%E5%89%8D-%E5%90%91%E8%91%A3%E4%BA%8B%E6%9C%83%E6%8A%AB%E9%9C%B2%E6%9B%B4%E5%A4%9A%E5%A9%9A%E5%A4%96%E6%83%85-143049325.html\"},\"canvass\":{\"count\":17},\"categoryLabel\":\"$undefined\",\"clickThroughUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.stock.yahoo.com/news/%E5%B0%8F%E4%BA%94%E6%9C%AA%E8%AD%89%E5%AF%A6%E5%8F%88%E5%82%B3%E6%9C%89%E5%B0%8F%E5% 向董事會披露更多婚外情\",\"photoVariants\":\"$undefined\",\"provider\":{\"displayName\":\"鉅亨網\",\"id\":\"$undefined\",\"logo\":{\"resolutions\":[]},\"sourceId\":\"$undefined\",\"url\":\"$undefined\"},\"providerContentUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"pubDate\":\"2026-11-24T14:30:49Z\",\"summary\":\"輝達23日宣布董事會一致投票恢復黃仁勳總裁、執行長及董事職務，股價當日飆漲逾31%，市值回補近9520億美元。 《華爾街日報》稱，黃仁勳22日與董事會閉門長談數小時，進一步披露更多未公開感情關係並表達悔意。 輝達盤前慘跌逾5％\",\"photoVariants\":\"$undefined\",\"provider\":{\"displayName\":\"中時財經即時\",\"id\":\"$undefined\",\"logo\":{\"resolutions\":[]},\"sourceId\":\"$undefined\",\"url\":\"$undefined\"},\"providerContentUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"pubDate\":\"2026-11-24T11:31:25Z\",\"summary\":\"黃仁勳回歸輝達執行長後，該公司單日大漲31.48%，台股今（24）天也受到激勵，猛漲3310.46點，收在40122.35點。然而，周二輝達盤前下跌超過5%，部分AI企業也出現下滑的趨勢。台指期夜盤稍早雖延續漲勢，一度上漲576點，報40900點，隨後卻一路震盪走低，一度暴跌988點，報39336點，失守4萬點大關，截至晚間8點左右，下跌624點，暫報39700點。\",\"tags\":[],\"thumbnail\":{\"crop\":\"$undefined\",\"originalHeight\":\"$undefined\",\"originalUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"originalWidth\":\"$undefined\",\"resolutions\":[]},\"title\":\"快訊》台指期夜盤失守4萬大關！一度重摔988點 輝達盤前慘跌逾5％\"},\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"id\":\"b8dbda5f-76b9-3261-8f32-76ed39f050c9\"},{\"content\":{\"author\":\"$undefined\",\"canonicalUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.stock.yahoo.com/news/%E9%BB%83%E4%BB%81%E5%8B%B3%E5%9B%9E%E4%BE%86%E4%BA%86-2018%E4%B9%9F%E5%9B%9E%E4%BE%86%E4%BA%86-%E9%AB%98%E7%9B%9B%E8%AD%A6%E5%91%8A-%E7%BE%8E%E8%82%A1%E6%9A%B4%E6%BC%B2%E5%8F%AA%E6%98%AF%E7%86%8A%E5%B8%82%E5%8F%8D%E5%BD%88-215005208.html\"},\"canvass\":{\"count\":1},\"categoryLabel\":\"$undefined\",\"clickThroughUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.stock.yahoo.com/news/%E9%BB%83%E4%BB%81%E5%8B%B3%E5%9B%9E%E4%BE%86%E4%BA%86-2018%E4%B9%9F%E5%9B%9E%E4%BE%86%E4%BA%86-%E9%AB%98%E7%9B%9B%E8%AD%A6%E5%91%8A-%E7%BE%8E%E8%82%A1%E6%9A%B4%E6%BC%B2%E5%8F%AA%E6%98%AF%E7%86%8A%E5%B8%82%E5%8F%8D%E5%BD%88-215005208.html\"},\"color\":\"$undefined\",\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"duration\":\"$undefined\",\"id\":\"481588b3-6f4c-3834-9a5c-b54511fa2de8\",\"isHosted\":true,\"label\":\"$undefined\",\"liveVideo\":\"$undefined\",\"mabLogString\":\"$undefined\",\"originalTitle\":\"黃仁勳回來了 2018也回來了！高盛警告：美股暴漲只是熊市反彈\",\"photoVariants\":\"$undefined\",\"provider\":{\"displayName\":\"鉅亨網\",\"id\":\"$undefined\",\"logo\":{\"resolutions\":[]},\"sourceId\":\"$undefined\",\"url\":\"$undefined\"},\"providerContentUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"pubDate\":\"2026-11-24T21:50:05Z\",\"summary\":\"黃仁勳回任輝達執行長帶動美股強彈、那指100漲約7%，但高盛指這波更像空頭回補的熊市反彈。 高盛稱信用市場落後股市，投資級公司債利差僅擴大約13個基點，2年期通膨交換利率降至2.09%。 花旗拿2026年對照2018年，稱景氣循環股、科技股與醫療保健轉弱，市場壓力恐持續擴散。\",\"tags\":[],\"thumbnail\":{\"crop\":\"$undefined\",\"originalHeight\":\"$undefined\",\"originalUrl\":\"https://media.zenfs.com/ko/cnyes.com.tw/f61cb7ac8276b1f526af952fa1ef190f.jpg\",\"originalWidth\":\"$undefined\",\"resolutions\":[{\"height\":280,\"tag\":\"498x280\",\"transformLabel\":\"498x280\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/BAFF95CE24B30C42466F203A04884322F38B7156E939752E41444108794FA764/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w498_h280_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fcnyes.com.tw%2Ff61cb7ac8276b1f526af952fa1ef190f.jpg\",\"width\":498},{\"height\":184,\"tag\":\"184x184\",\"transformLabel\":\"184x184\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/3835BBE61E11FFA1C7281E0C02DC19A6116A0F97FAC688081F6EEFC602AFAFD4/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w184_h184_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fcnyes.com.tw%2Ff61cb7ac8276b1f526af952fa1ef190f.jpg\",\"width\":184}]},\"title\":\"黃仁勳回來了 135萬名勞工退休金變多\",\"photoVariants\":\"$undefined\",\"provider\":{\"displayName\":\"聯合新聞網\",\"id\":\"$undefined\",\"logo\":{\"resolutions\":[]},\"sourceId\":\"$undefined\",\"url\":\"$undefined\"},\"providerContentUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"pubDate\":\"2026-11-24T22:32:34Z\",\"summary\":\"2027年元旦起，最低工資月薪將調漲至30,900元。配合最低工資調整，勞動部公告「勞工退休金月提繳分級表」，現行第26級月提繳工資，將由30,300元，修正為30,900元，同樣自2027年1月1日起生效。另配合修正現行第27級實際工資級距下限，其他級距維持不變。\",\"tags\":[],\"thumbnail\":{\"crop\":\"$undefined\",\"originalHeight\":\"$undefined\",\"originalUrl\":\"https://media.zenfs.com/ko/udn.com/497bfaac7beb7505e8849954968ef33b.jpg\",\"originalWidth\":\"$undefined\",\"resolutions\":[{\"height\":280,\"tag\":\"498x280\",\"transformLabel\":\"498x280\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/10DE2A159DE5F4C44D833E112DF1667C576E0CED60CE28F33D266527B7AAB990/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w498_h280_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fudn.com%2F497bfaac7beb7505e8849954968ef33b.jpg\",\"width\":498},{\"height\":184,\"tag\":\"184x184\",\"transformLabel\":\"184x184\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/32E324DB1A2D1B9B8D65945F1DEF97BB7ABD1FC54B5B3CD6D10BF96E68F82793/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w184_h184_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fudn.com%2F497bfaac7beb7505e8849954968ef33b.jpg\",\"width\":184}]},\"title\":\"最低工資明年調漲效應…勞退級距調整 台積電跌190元\",\"photoVariants\":\"$undefined\",\"provider\":{\"displayName\":\"Yahoo即時新聞\",\"id\":\"$undefined\",\"logo\":{\"resolutions\":[]},\"sourceId\":\"$undefined\",\"url\":\"$undefined\"},\"providerContentUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"pubDate\":\"2026-11-25T01:06:50Z\",\"summary\":\"由於人工智慧（AI）熱潮前景不明，科技類股再次遭投資人拋售，美國股市四大指數多收低。台北股市今天（25日）早盤重挫逾3100點，加權指數最低為37010.49點，4萬點大關失守，跌破年線約39554點。台積電最多跌190元至2040元。\",\"tags\":[],\"thumbnail\":{\"crop\":\"$undefined\",\"originalHeight\":\"$undefined\",\"originalUrl\":\"https://media.zenfs.com/zh-tw/cna.com.tw/6d4b91972ecd348193a27fe4c10a482d.jpg\",\"originalWidth\":\"$undefined\",\"resolutions\":[{\"height\":280,\"tag\":\"498x280\",\"transformLabel\":\"498x280\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/893F12B05FD681425BD38B9ED47017EA4B4F94242DB147868BA758CF037F8B12/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w498_h280_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fzh-tw%2Fcna.com.tw%2F6d4b91972ecd348193a27fe4c10a482d.jpg\",\"width\":498},{\"height\":184,\"tag\":\"184x184\",\"transformLabel\":\"184x184\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/8783C6C44DDAAEEF3C4991E4619CE7F236745B275A2517D0A49A17AF71A426B4/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w184_h184_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fzh-tw%2Fcna.com.tw%2F6d4b91972ecd348193a27fe4c10a482d.jpg\",\"width\":184}]},\"title\":\"台股早盤重挫逾3100點跌破年線 台積電跌190元\"},\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"id\":\"e8a92207-4484-4ac0-b225-89c83c41f60d\"},{\"content\":{\"author\":\"$undefined\",\"canonicalUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.news.yahoo.com/%E7%9B%A4%E5%89%8D-%E5%B7%9D%E6%99%AE%E9%97%9C%E7%A8%85%E5%86%8D%E8%B5%B7%E8%B2%BB%E5%8D%8A%E5%B4%A9%E8%BF%918-%E5%8F%B0%E6%8C%87%E5%A4%9C%E7%9B%A4%E6%AE%BA1842%E9%BB%9E-%E5%8F%B0%E8%82%A14%E8%90%AC%E9%BB%9E%E5%91%8A%E6%80%A5-222800418.html\"},\"canvass\":{\"count\":79},\"categoryLabel\":\"$undefined\",\"clickThroughUrl\":{\"url\":\"https://tw.news.yahoo.com/%E7%9B%A4%E5%89%8D-%E5%B7%9D%E6%99%AE%E9%97%9C%E7%A8%85%E5%86%8D%E8%B5%B7%E8%B2%BB%E5%8D%8A%E5%B4%A9%E8%BF%918-%E5%8F%B0%E6%8C%87%E5%A4%9C%E7%9B%A4%E6%AE%BA1842%E9%BB%9E-%E5%8F%B0%E8%82%A14%E8%90%AC%E9%BB%9E%E5%91%8A%E6%80%A5-222800418.html\"},\"color\":\"$undefined\",\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"duration\":\"$undefined\",\"id\":\"f1928ffd-e8ca-30a0-8cbe-b4d9ac5586aa\",\"isHosted\":true,\"label\":\"$undefined\",\"liveVideo\":\"$undefined\",\"mabLogString\":\"$undefined\",\"originalTitle\":\"【盤前】川普關稅再起費半崩近8%！台指夜盤殺1842點　台股4萬點告急\",\"photoVariants\":\"$undefined\",\"provider\":{\"displayName\":\"三立新聞網 setn.com\",\"id\":\"$undefined\",\"logo\":{\"resolutions\":[]},\"sourceId\":\"$undefined\",\"url\":\"$undefined\"},\"providerContentUrl\":\"$undefined\",\"pubDate\":\"2026-11-24T22:28:00Z\",\"summary\":\"美股週二（24日）主要指數漲跌互見，前一交易日由黃仁勳回任輝達執行長帶動的AI股反彈行情未能延續，加上美國總統川普新一輪關稅計畫及聯準會（Fed）升息疑慮，科技股再度遭遇沉重賣壓，半導體及記憶體族群成為重災區。費半重挫7.88%，台指期夜盤大跌1842點，台股今（25）日開盤恐面臨4萬點保衛戰。\",\"tags\":[],\"thumbnail\":{\"crop\":\"$undefined\",\"originalHeight\":\"$undefined\",\"originalUrl\":\"https://media.zenfs.com/ko/setn.com.tw/934d24768f898d5ad511b0235c610fc4.jpg\",\"originalWidth\":\"$undefined\",\"resolutions\":[{\"height\":280,\"tag\":\"498x280\",\"transformLabel\":\"498x280\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/35239DB0177E3DF4FF7C964A6ED8DC34EF12414BB4D331E9EBE04760EBF5A740/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w498_h280_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fsetn.com.tw%2F934d24768f898d5ad511b0235c610fc4.jpg\",\"width\":498},{\"height\":184,\"tag\":\"184x184\",\"transformLabel\":\"184x184\",\"url\":\"https://s.yimg.com/lo/mysterio/api/D0DF6000454A4570C2DD454947184AC3CBE716DFB89EA9C07539E752AF141DDF/subgraphmysterio/smartcrop_w184_h184_faces;quality_80;format_webp/https:%2F%2Fmedia.zenfs.com%2Fko%2Fsetn.com.tw%2F934d24768f898d5ad511b0235c610fc4.jpg\",\"width\":184}]},\"title\":\"【盤前】川普關稅再起費半崩近8%！台指夜盤殺1842點　台股4萬點告急\"},\"contentType\":\"Story\",\"id\":\"f1928ffd-e8ca-30a0-8cbe-b4d9ac5586aa\"}]}],\"\"]}]\n"])</script></body></html>
